- AFP Action: Mar 4, 2026; Skylar Zander: "We are glad that Congressman Donalds wants to protect Florida's 2023 tort reform laws"; priorities: housing and insurance reform, keeping the 2023 tort reforms, shrinking Citizens, school choice (Hope Scholarship), fiscal responsibility., [AFP Action](https://afpaction.com/afp-action-endorses-byron-donalds-for-governor/)
- Associated Builders and Contractors of Florida: April 14, 2026 (campaign site listing)., [byrondonalds.com](https://byrondonalds.com/associated-builders-and-contractors-of-florida-endorse-byron-donalds-for-governor/)
- Florida Chamber of Commerce: June 23, 2026, Tampa; Mark Wilson: "Florida is changing... nothing less than Florida's future is at stake"; priorities: workforce training, competitive economic climate, high-wage jobs, education., [Tampa Free Press](https://www.tampafp.com/byron-donalds-lands-powerhouse-florida-chamber-endorsement-for-governor/); [campaign release](https://byrondonalds.com/florida-chamber-of-commerce-endorses-byron-donalds-for-governor/). At the same event Donalds rejected a utility ROI cap (see KQ3).
- Florida Realtors PAC: Aug 21, 2026; chair Scott Steger cites "protecting private property rights," homeownership, the real estate industry., [Florida's Voice](https://flvoicenews.com/florida-realtors-pac-endorses-donalds-simpson-and-statewide-gop-candidates-in-2026-races/). Florida Realtors put $10M into "Yes On 3" (Amendment 3 property tax cut) the week before Sept 10, 2026; opponent Edie Ousley argues realtors benefit from "more real estate transactions at higher prices.", [Florida Trend / NSF, Sept 10, 2026](https://www.floridatrend.com/articles/2026/09/10/realtors-back-property-tax-cut-measure/)
- Florida Farm Bureau FarmPAC: endorsed (Florida Politics headline, c. June 2026; page could not be fetched)., [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/802899-byron-donalds-harvests-florida-farm-bureau-farmpacs-endorsement-for-governor/)
- Law enforcement: Florida Police Chiefs Association (July 2026); 17 additional sheriffs (Aug 2026) on top of Judd, Ivey, Nocco, Waters, Chronister, Cordero-Stutz; Florida State FOP (24,000 members, early Sept 2026); Florida PBA (31,000 members, Sept 14, 2026) citing "competitive pay and benefits" and "a real seat at the table in Tallahassee.", [The Floridian, Sept 14, 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/09/floridas-largest-police-union-endorses-byron-donalds-for-governor/)
- 13 county property appraisers: Aug 4, 2026., [Hoodline](https://hoodline.com/2026/08/13-florida-property-appraisers-back-byron-donalds-for-governor-as-property-tax-war-erupts/)
- SBA Pro-Life America: July 20, 2026., [campaign site](https://byrondonalds.com/susan-b-anthony-pro-life-america-endorses-byrondonalds/)
- DeSantis: Sept 1, 2026 at the Florida GOP Leadership Forum, TPepin's Hospitality Centre, Tampa: "I'm endorsing the slate here tonight" (Donalds, Uthmeier, Ingoglia, Simpson). Donalds: "My mission as the state's next governor is to build on the success of Ron DeSantis.", [NBC Miami, Sept 2, 2026](https://www.nbcmiami.com/news/local/gov-desantis-endorses-fellow-republican-byron-donalds-for-governor/3853840/); [WUSF, Sept 3, 2026](https://wusf.org/text/politics-issues/2026-09-03/desantis-endorses-donalds-florida-governor-despite-past-tensions)
- Everglades Trust: Oct 6, 2026, Miami; CEO Anna Upton: "he is the only candidate who can maintain the momentum of Governor DeSantis when it comes to Everglades restoration"; credits him with the Lake Okeechobee regulation schedule. Donalds: "committing to finishing Everglades restoration in my time as governor." Sugar and the EAA reservoir not mentioned., [The Floridian, Oct 6, 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/10/everglades-trust-endorses-byron-donalds-for-governor/)
- 40 Florida rabbis: Oct 16, 2026, announced in Naples; campaign Jewish outreach director Lisa Spies: "Donalds stood with the Jewish community and Israel when it mattered most.", [Hoodline, Oct 16, 2026](https://hoodline.com/2026/10/40-florida-rabbis-back-byron-donalds-for-governor-weeks-before-election/)
- Heritage: Erika Donalds became a Heritage Foundation visiting fellow in education choice on Nov 14, 2023., [Heritage](https://www.heritage.org/press/erika-donalds-joins-heritage-visiting-fellow-education-freedom)

##### Inferences
- The AIF and Chamber endorsements function partly as conduits: their committees are financed by sugar, FPL, HCA, Disney and data center interests, so "business community" support carries those specific industries' money even where they do not give directly.

##### Gaps
- No source found confirming a formal Florida Medical Association, Moms for Liberty or Heritage Action endorsement of Donalds for governor (Moms for Liberty's co-founder base is in Florida but no endorsement article surfaced). Treat as unconfirmed.
- Florida Politics pages on Chamber, Realtors and Farm Bureau endorsements returned client errors; details above come from mirror coverage.

#### Key Question 3: What he says he will do as governor, and who gains or loses

##### Takeaway
His agenda is Amendment 3 now and homestead property tax elimination via the 2027 Tax and Budget Reform Commission; a "Bring Down the Bill" insurance plan that keeps the 2022 and 2023 tort reforms and shrinks Citizens; conditional support for data centers and opposition to any utility ROI cap or moratorium; universal voucher expansion with a heavy ed-tech and private sector role; removal of local officials over sanctuary policies and E-Verify for all businesses; transparency audits rather than a named DOGE. Analysts say property tax cuts shift burden to local governments and non-homestead property, and his insurance plan favors the existing carrier-friendly framework.

##### Cited Findings

Property taxes
- Supports Amendment 3 (homestead exemption to $150,000 in 2027, $250,000 in 2028, indexed from 2029; non-homestead assessment cap from 10% to 5%; needs 60%). Says: "Pass or fail, we're bringing this back anyway next year," via the Tax and Budget Reform Commission convening in 2027, aiming at full elimination of homestead property taxes, with fallback relief targeted to seniors and working families., [WLRN/WUSF, Sept 1, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/government-politics/2026-09-01/byron-donalds-doubles-down-on-cutting-or-even-eliminating-property-taxes-if-elected); [CBS Miami, July 5, 2026](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/byron-donalds-speaks-on-his-stance-regarding-property-taxes-and-alligator-alcatraz/)
- Who loses: Florida Policy Institute estimates nearly $12B a year in local revenue loss; municipalities might raise taxes on rentals and commercial property., [WLRN, Sept 1, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/government-politics/2026-09-01/byron-donalds-doubles-down-on-cutting-or-even-eliminating-property-taxes-if-elected). A legislative analysis puts the first full year non-school revenue cut at nearly $5B., [WPTV, Aug 20, 2026](https://www.wptv.com/news/state/donalds-promises-insurance-cuts-defends-data-centers-as-race-pivots-to-november). Florida Association of Special Districts (1,900 districts for fire, mosquito control, hospitals) gave $100,000 to Vote No on 3; rural officials say it undercuts basic services., [Florida Trend, Sept 10, 2026](https://www.floridatrend.com/articles/2026/09/10/realtors-back-property-tax-cut-measure/)
- Who gains: homestead owners; Florida Realtors ($10M to Yes On 3). Donalds floated "a multi-tiered taxing system" without detail., [WPTV, Aug 20, 2026](https://www.wptv.com/news/state/donalds-promises-insurance-cuts-defends-data-centers-as-race-pivots-to-november)
- Florida Politics reported Donalds narrowed the scope of a prospective break if Amendment 3 fails, citing a "math" problem (page not fetchable)., [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/814724-donalds-math-amendment-3/)

Property insurance
- "Bring Down the Bill" (unveiled week of Sept 14, 2026): cut the Cat Fund surcharge when reserves are strong and let insurers pool reinsurance; keep the 2022 and 2023 lawsuit-limiting reforms; target My Safe Florida Home grants to pre-1992 homes; create an "Insurer Scorecard" on pricing, denial rates and payment timing; keep shrinking Citizens (1.4M policies late 2023 to about 274,000 by June 2026); permitting reform, building material mandate review, reciprocal contractor licensing; optional binding arbitration; affiliate payment rules; plain language disclosure. Goal: "bring costs down another 20 to 25 percent.", [WLRN, Sept 17, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/government-politics/2026-09-17/jolly-and-donalds-clash-on-property-insurance-proposals); [InsuranceNewsNet](https://insurancenewsnet.com/oarticle/uncovered-how-floridas-governor-and-cfo-candidates-plan-to-fix-your-property-insurance)
- Jolly's alternative: convert the Cat Fund into a primary state wind insurer, claimed 60 to 70% savings; Donalds' ads call it a "$1,000 hurricane tax." PolitiFact: the ad cites an FSU/OPPAGA study of a different 2024 proposal; the $1,000 figure is one post-catastrophe assessment scenario; Jolly's savings claims are also doubtful. Experts: UCF's Gabriel Carrillo ("The risk would then be borne by all consumers, not just homeowners"), Jeff Brandes., [PolitiFact, Sept 21, 2026](https://politifact.com/article/2026/sep/21/jolly-donalds-hurricane-insurance-1000-florida/)
- Who gains: private carriers keep wind business and the 2022-23 tort framework; AFP Action explicitly praised his pledge to protect the 2023 tort reforms., [AFP Action](https://afpaction.com/afp-action-endorses-byron-donalds-for-governor/). Sen. Tracie Davis says the reforms favor insurers; Ingoglia cites a state actuarial projection of 72% higher premiums if repealed., [InsuranceNewsNet](https://insurancenewsnet.com/oarticle/uncovered-how-floridas-governor-and-cfo-candidates-plan-to-fix-your-property-insurance)
- Insurance donors: State Farm Federal PAC $14,000 to his House account (2026 cycle)., [FundVoter](https://fundvoter.org/politicians/byron-donalds). No source found itemizing property insurer donations to the governor PAC.

Utilities and energy
- June 23, 2026, Tampa (after the Chamber endorsement): opposes capping utility return on equity: "a cap might sound good politically, but it's a terrible solution," comparing it to NYC rent control; favors small modular nuclear, hydrogen and another natural gas pipeline. CLEO Institute: Donalds has received $1.79M from Florida's major utilities over his career, second only to Wilton Simpson among state leaders. Context: PSC approved a $6.9B (up to $7B over four years) FPL rate increase on Nov 20, 2025 with a 10.95% ROE vs about 9.7% national average. Jolly would cap ROE at the national average., [Florida Phoenix via News From The States, June 23, 2026](https://www.newsfromthestates.com/article/donalds-and-jolly-bicker-over-response-utility-profitability); [Tallahassee Democrat via AOL](https://www.aol.com/articles/fpl-rate-hike-governor-candidates-100330108.html)
- NextEra Energy gave the PAC $500,000 by Jan 2026 and $1M by July 2026; Duke Energy $500,000; TECO is also a top data center donor., [News4Jax, Jan 15, 2026](https://www.news4jax.com/news/politics/2026/01/15/rep-byron-donalds-continues-to-hold-wide-fundraising-advantage-in-floridas-race-for-governor-here-are-his-top-donors/); [WGCU, July 1, 2026](https://www.wgcu.org/housing/2026-07-01/airbnb-gives-1-million-to-byron-donalds-as-rentals-remain-a-suncoast-flashpoint); [VoteWater](https://votewater.org/dirty-money-talks-what-does-it-say-about-byron-donalds/); [Seeking Rents, Oct 15, 2026](https://jasongarcia.substack.com/p/data-center-interests-have-put-30)

Data centers and AI
- Conditional support; opposes a New York-style moratorium ("that's a signal to the business community that this is not a place to invest capital"); says rates cannot rise because of a hyperscale center and wants setback rules from homes; backs existing law leaving siting to counties. A July 2026 UNF poll: 68% of Florida voters oppose data centers in their area. Jolly pledges a one-year moratorium on day one., [Orlando Sentinel via GovTech, Oct 19, 2026](https://www.govtech.com/artificial-intelligence/how-data-centers-are-impacting-floridas-political-campaigns); [Fox49, Aug 14, 2026](https://fox49.tv/news/local/byron-donalds-where-the-florida-governor-candidate-stands-on-the-issues)
- Money: data center boosters have given Donalds more than $6M in direct donations and ad support; American Mission Florida (chaired by Sarasota Property Appraiser Bill Furst, registered Feb 2026, address a Tallahassee Staples) spent more than $2M on pro-Donalds primary ads, funded solely by a $3M March 4, 2026 transfer from Leading the Future, the federal super PAC backed by Andreessen Horowitz ($25M), Marc Andreessen and Ben Horowitz ($12.5M each), and Greg and Anna Brockman ($25M); Leading the Future said it would spend $5M for Donalds. Google, Meta and Amazon each gave more than $400,000 to Florida candidates and committees. NextEra's CEO said a major Florida data center project will be announced by year-end. AI and data center regulation is expected to top the 2027 session., [Seeking Rents, Oct 15, 2026](https://jasongarcia.substack.com/p/data-center-interests-have-put-30); [Suncoast Searchlight via WUSF, Apr 19, 2026](https://wusf.org/text/politics-issues/2026-04-19/sarasota-property-appraiser-moving-millions-ai-moguls-support-byron-donalds). UCF's Aubrey Jewett called PAC-to-PAC transfers "a form of political money laundering."
- Jolly, Oct 19, 2026 (first day of early voting), Orlando: Donalds is "funded with over $10 million of data center money" and "a vessel for every special interest that's controlled this state.", [Orlando Sentinel via The Derrick](https://www.thederrick.com/news/entertainment/david-jolly-in-orlando-blasts-byron-donalds-over-support-for-data-centers/article_73378862-2898-5d8a-ac4a-4d0283e77e18.html)
- Personal holdings: up to $390,000 in data-center-linked stocks (Microsoft, Amazon, Monolithic Power Systems) per his 2025 disclosure; Erika Donalds reported income from X Corp. Donalds: "I don't tell him what to trade or what not to trade." Campaign Legal Center's Kedric Payne: that "does not in any way remove the conflict of interest.", [Mother Jones, Oct 15, 2026](https://www.motherjones.com/politics/2026/10/byron-donalds-is-pro-data-centers-so-is-his-stock-portfolio/)

Education
- Plan: parents "at the head of the table"; replace A-F grading and annual testing with individualized learning plans for all students; modular grades 6 to 12; apprenticeships; heavy AI and ed-tech role; Erika Donalds: "Profit is not going to be a bad word when it comes to the education industry." Context: universal vouchers (2023) cost about $4.5B a year for more than 500,000 students at roughly $8,000 each; most voucher students attend Christian private schools (Orlando Sentinel). FEA sued in May 2026. Donalds statement: "Donors do not set my agenda, the people of Florida do"; "No donor has asked me to pursue school choice as governor because they don't have to.", [Florida Trib via FlaglerLive, Aug 5, 2026](https://flaglerlive.com/gospel-of-school-choice/)
- Erika Donalds, Oct 2026: vouchers should be funded from the same pot as public and charter schools ("Absolutely, yes"); local control has limits; expects Tallahassee to revisit the Live Local Act., [WUSF/WLRN, Oct 19, 2026](https://www.wusf.org/politics-issues/2026-10-19/erika-donalds-weighs-in-on-immigration-school-vouchers-state-power-over-local-governments)
- Who gains: Griffin ($10M) and Yass ($7.5M) both back school choice; Erika Donalds' OptimaEd and Optima Management Solutions hold taxpayer-funded charter contracts (see KQ5)., [FlaglerLive](https://flaglerlive.com/gospel-of-school-choice/); [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-billionaire-trying-to-reinvent-florida-schools-has-a-7-5-million-bet-on-byron-donalds-40565603/)

Immigration
- Aug 12, 2026, Tampa: will remove local officials who "create sanctuaries" and suspend state attorneys who "go rogue"; "Zero Tolerance, Zero Dollars" withholds state grants from non-compliant entities and audits state contracts; campaign plan requires E-Verify for all businesses, cuts off benefits, coordinates deportations, targets fentanyl., [Florida Phoenix, Aug 12, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/08/12/byron-donalds-says-hell-remove-local-officials-who-indulge-in-sanctuary-policies/); [Fox49](https://fox49.tv/news/local/byron-donalds-where-the-florida-governor-candidate-stands-on-the-issues); [campaign plan](https://byrondonalds.com/zero-tolerance-zero-dollars/)
- Alligator Alcatraz: "mission accomplished"; will pursue full federal reimbursement (about $58M of an estimated $1.2B received as of July 2026); said in May 2026 it is "probably not" still needed., [WFLX, June 10, 2026](https://www.wflx.com/2026/06/10/byron-donalds-calls-florida-immigration-crackdown-mission-accomplished-seeks-federal-repayment/); [CBS Miami, July 5, 2026](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/byron-donalds-speaks-on-his-stance-regarding-property-taxes-and-alligator-alcatraz/); [The Floridian, May 9, 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/05/byron-donalds-backs-preliminary-talks-to-close-alligator-alcatraz/)
- Who gains: detention vendors owed $417M by FDEM (see KQ5).

Spending and "DOGE"
- May 6, 2026 "Most Transparent Government in America" plan: expand audits, centralize financial records, public spending portal. No agency cuts named., [campaign release](https://byrondonalds.com/byron-donalds-unveils-plan-to-make-florida-the-most-transparent-government-in-america/). Says "Citizens aren't a piggy bank for government"; efficiency reviews, a formula for 29 fiscally constrained counties, more tourist cost-sharing., [Fox49](https://fox49.tv/news/local/byron-donalds-where-the-florida-governor-candidate-stands-on-the-issues)
- Florida DOGE itself is a DeSantis creation (Feb 24, 2025)., [Destin Log](https://www.thedestinlog.com/story/news/local/state/2025/02/24/desantis-starts-florida-doge-to-look-at-job-cuts-university-audits/80021362007/)

Other
- Abortion: keep current law. Guns: "Second Amendment absolutist," repeal the under-21 purchase ban., [Fox49](https://fox49.tv/news/local/byron-donalds-where-the-florida-governor-candidate-stands-on-the-issues)
- Vacation rentals: as a state representative in 2020 he called the industry "a boon to Southwest Florida" and backed a bill blocking local vacation rental regulation. Airbnb gave $1.5M on May 19, 2026 to "The People Versus The Powerful" (chaired by Airbnb's Southeast policy lead Andy Gonzalez), which transferred $1M to the Donalds PAC the same day., [WGCU, July 1, 2026](https://www.wgcu.org/housing/2026-07-01/airbnb-gives-1-million-to-byron-donalds-as-rentals-remain-a-suncoast-flashpoint)

##### Inferences
- The Realtors' $10M for Amendment 3 and their endorsement line up with Donalds' tax elimination pitch; the losers (special districts, rural counties, renters and commercial owners who absorb shifted burden) have far less money in the race.
- On insurance, every element of his plan preserves the structure insurers won in 2022 and 2023; the "scorecard" is the only element that imposes anything on carriers.

##### Gaps
- No analyst breakdown found of which specific insurers or reinsurers gave to the governor PAC.
- Could not fetch the Florida Politics "math problem" piece on narrowing the property tax plan.

#### Key Question 4: Relationship with Ron and Casey DeSantis and with Trump as of October 2026; which donors sided with each

##### Takeaway
DeSantis spent 2025 trying to block Donalds (lobbyist calls, "hasn't been a part of any of the victories," floating Casey, installing Jay Collins) but never fielded Casey, declined to endorse through the primary, and endorsed the slate including Donalds on Sept 1, 2026. Trump endorsed Feb 20, 2025, defended Donalds in Sept 2026 after the campaign scrubbed Trump from its website ("If Florida thought the story that Byron 'dropped' me was true, he would lose the Election"), and is rallying with him in Fort Myers Oct 23. Several former DeSantis megadonors (Uihlein, Yass, Griffin, the Seminole Tribe, Clive Fields) moved to Donalds.

##### Cited Findings
- Trump endorsement Feb 20, 2025 (see KQ2). DeSantis, Feb 2025: "A guy like Byron, he just hasn't been a part of any of the victories"; proposed Casey DeSantis as a successor who "would do better than me.", [MS NOW](https://www.ms.now/top-stories/latest/ron-casey-desantis-trump-florida-governor-election-byron-donalds-rcna193710); [NBC Miami](https://www.nbcmiami.com/news/local/gov-desantis-endorses-fellow-republican-byron-donalds-for-governor/3853840/)
- March 2025: DeSantis and political aide Taryn Fenske reportedly called Tallahassee lobbyists urging them not to support Donalds ("keep your powder dry"). Donors to Donalds at that point: Richard Uihlein $1M, Clive Fields (Texas health care exec, former DeSantis donor turned Trump backer) $500,000, Deborah Magowan $150,000., [NBC News](https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/elections/ron-desantis-florida-lobbyists-rep-byron-donalds-governor-rcna196996)
- Later in 2025: DeSantis staffers Anastasios Kamoutsas and Peter Cuderman reportedly asked lobbyists to pledge money to a DeSantis-aligned committee for a possible Casey run; both denied it; Integrity Florida's Ben Wilcox criticized use of taxpayer-funded staff., [NBC News](https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/elections/ron-desantis-lobbyist-fundraising-wifes-governor-campaign-rcna198380)
- Dec 2024 context: the "swift falling out" began after Donalds backed Trump over DeSantis in the 2024 presidential primary; Donalds had introduced DeSantis at his 2022 election night party. Donalds advisers: Ryan Smith, Brad Herold, Tony Fabrizio, Megan McKinley., [NBC News, Dec 2024](https://www.nbcnews.com/politics/politics-news/rep-byron-donalds-gubernatorial-bid-2026-rcna187721)
- 2023 flashpoint: Donalds criticized a line in Florida's Black history standards; DeSantis's team hit back., [WUSF, Sept 3, 2026](https://wusf.org/text/politics-issues/2026-09-03/desantis-endorses-donalds-florida-governor-despite-past-tensions)
- Casey DeSantis never entered; by August 2025 when DeSantis named Jay Collins lieutenant governor she was no longer considering it. DeSantis said in 2025 he would not back Donalds, called Renner's entry "ill-advised," and declined to endorse anyone in the primary., [Florida Phoenix, Aug 6, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/08/06/gop-gubernatorial-primary-election-seems-a-foregone-conclusion/). Tampa Bay Times (July 24, 2026): DeSantis had not endorsed a successor; Casey chose not to run., [AOL/TBT](https://www.aol.com/articles/billionaire-ken-griffin-pours-money-192303000.html)
- DeSantis also criticized RPOF debate rules (10% polling, $10M raised, 10,000 donors) as designed to clear the field for Donalds; Fishback called Donalds "bought and paid for by out-of-state billionaires.", [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-tiktok-billionaire-bankrolling-byron-donalds-40558946/)
- Primary result Aug 18, 2026: Donalds 811,131 (48%), Collins 427,014 (25%), Fishback 177,809 (10%), Renner 145,063 (9%). Jolly won the Democratic primary with 61%., [ClickOrlando](https://www.clickorlando.com/election-2026/2026/08/18/election-results-for-governor-and-other-statewide-races-in-the-florida-primary-on-aug-18-2026/). Donalds lost 17 counties to Collins., [Florida Phoenix, Oct 6, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/10/06/new-ad-targets-byron-donalds-in-the-counties-he-lost-in-the-gop-primary/)
- DeSantis endorsement Sept 1, 2026 (see KQ2). Donalds now runs ads featuring both Trump and DeSantis endorsements and warns Jolly "will erase DeSantis' agenda.", [CBS Miami, Oct 6, 2026](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/byron-donalds-new-ads-florida-governor-race-david-jolly/); [The Floridian, Oct 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/10/byron-donalds-says-david-jolly-will-erase-desantis-agenda-if-elected-governor/)
- Hope Florida: After CBS Miami obtained the leaked grand jury report finding the administration "misappropriated" $10M of a Medicaid settlement for political purposes (money routed to defeat the 2024 marijuana amendment via a committee run by then chief of staff James Uthmeier), Donalds called the findings "very concerning," backed routing settlement funds to general revenue and guardrails on outside groups, but said the matter had been investigated and that Jolly's call for a new probe was "just playing politics." Jolly: "This is money laundering"; "I think she [Casey DeSantis] was used." An appeals court struck the report on Oct 11, 2026; DeSantis declared victory., [WUSF, Sept 1, 2026](https://www.wusf.org/politics-issues/2026-09-01/donalds-jolly-call-hope-florida-reforms-grand-jury-report); [Tampa Bay Times via Brunswick News, Aug 30, 2026](https://thebrunswicknews.com/news/national_news/donalds-dismisses-jolly-s-call-for-new-hope-florida-investigation-as-politics/article_fa1ce673-f8d6-5848-8d1d-fd88e9851b3e.html); [Florida Phoenix, Aug 28, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/08/28/david-jolly-first-lady-casey-desantis-hope-florida-were-used-in-a-sinister-plan/); [CBS Miami, Oct 11, 2026](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/desantis-hope-florida-grand-jury-10-11-2026/)
- Trump website episode: Sept 22, 2026 the Guardian, NBC and Miami Herald reported Donalds' relaunched site removed at least eight Trump references including an "agenda" banner and a photo; campaign said the site focuses on policy. Trump approval cited at or below 30% in several trackers., [WGCU, Sept 23, 2026](https://www.wgcu.org/government-politics/2026-09-23/varied-media-sites-report-donalds-scrubs-references-to-trump-off-campaign-site). Trump on Truth Social Sept 26, 2026: "Another Fake Story is that a very close friend of mine, a man I endorsed very early..." and "If Florida thought the story that Byron 'dropped' me was true, he would lose the Election"; announced two Florida rallies with Donalds., [Raw Story, Sept 26, 2026](https://www.rawstory.com/trump-2677945568/); [WLT Report](https://wltreport.com/2026/09/26/president-trump-byron-donalds-website-story-two-florida-rallies/)
- Trump rally scheduled Friday Oct 23, 2026, Alico Arena, FGCU, Fort Myers, 2 p.m. UCF's Aubrey Jewett: aimed at base turnout; if the race "becomes a referendum just on the president," Donalds and Moody "are in some trouble.", [WINK, Oct 19 to 21, 2026](https://www.winknews.com/news/why-fort-myers-the-politics-behind-president-trumps-southwest-florida-rally/article_ab2f3e58-10da-4f2d-bb1d-8f80339cfb2b.html); [Florida National News, Oct 17, 2026](https://floridanationalnews.com/blog/trump-plans-fort-myers-rally-as-byron-donalds-seeks-to-become-floridas-first-black-governor/)
- Donors who moved from DeSantis to Donalds: Uihlein (about $2M to Friends of Ron DeSantis 2018 and 2022, $3M to Never Back Down; $1M to Donalds by March 2025, $2M by July 2026); Yass (more than $2.5M to Friends of Ron DeSantis; $7.5M to Donalds); Griffin (longtime DeSantis donor and ally; $10M to Donalds July 24, 2026); Seminole Tribe (longtime DeSantis supporter; $1M in Aug 2025 "possibly hedging" against a Casey or Collins run, $4M total by Aug 2026)., [News4Jax, Apr 14, 2025](https://gmg-wjxt-prod.cdn.arcpublishing.com/news/local/2025/04/14/byron-donalds-secures-6-million-from-influential-gop-donors-in-early-fundraising-for-florida-governors-race); [AOL/TBT, July 24, 2026](https://www.aol.com/articles/billionaire-ken-griffin-pours-money-192303000.html); [Florida Politics, Aug 2025](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/753415-seminole-tribe-antes-up-with-byron-donalds/); [WGCU](https://www.wgcu.org/housing/2026-07-01/airbnb-gives-1-million-to-byron-donalds-as-rentals-remain-a-suncoast-flashpoint)
- Donors who sided with DeSantis's camp: Propel Florida LLC gave $5M to Jay Collins (described as formed to combat Big Tech)., [WLRN, June 12, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/government-politics/2026-06-12/byron-donalds-continues-fundraising-domination-in-florida-governors-race). The DeSantis-linked Florida Freedom Fund held about $5M in reserve in mid-2025., [Yahoo/NSF](https://www.yahoo.com/news/byron-donalds-dwarfs-david-jolly-163544449.html). CDR Enterprises (an Alligator Alcatraz vendor) gave Collins $250,000 in Q4 2025 while CDR entities gave Donalds more than $620,000 overall., [News4Jax](https://www.news4jax.com/news/politics/2026/01/15/rep-byron-donalds-continues-to-hold-wide-fundraising-advantage-in-floridas-race-for-governor-here-are-his-top-donors/); [WUSF, Sept 30, 2026](https://www.wusf.org/politics-issues/2026-09-30/immigration-detention-money-flows-florida-governors-race)
- Polling in October: Stetson (Oct 1 to 9) Donalds 52, Jolly 41; Mason-Dixon 48 to 41; St. Pete Polls 45 to 45; CBS12 average 48.0 to 43.3. DGA put $1M behind Jolly on Oct 20, 2026., [Hoodline, Oct 16, 2026](https://hoodline.com/2026/10/40-florida-rabbis-back-byron-donalds-for-governor-weeks-before-election/); [Florida Phoenix, Oct 20, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/10/20/democratic-governors-association-investing-1-million-to-support-david-jolly/)

##### Inferences
- The donor migration shows the Florida establishment treating Donalds as the inevitable successor well before DeSantis conceded; DeSantis's Sept 1 endorsement reads as ratification rather than enthusiasm.
- The website scrub plus Trump's "he would lose" post suggest the campaign sees Trump as a general-election liability in suburban counties even as it needs his base turnout.

##### Gaps
- CNN's July 18, 2026 "family feud" piece and Florida Politics' Trump rally piece could not be fetched; no direct quote from Casey DeSantis on Donalds found.

#### Key Question 5: Documented conflicts and pay-to-play allegations

##### Takeaway
No source alleges illegality, but reporters have documented: the IRS letter and bitcoin purchase; a 2024 CLC STOCK Act complaint over 108 undisclosed trades (up to $1.6M); years of inaccurate financial disclosures amended in June and Aug 2026; his wife's for-profit charter firms taking more than $10M from charter schools she helped found while he promotes voucher and charter expansion; detention vendors owed $417M giving more than $3.3M; gambling and prediction market money (more than $6M) ahead of a looming compact fight; sugar money routed through conduits; Airbnb routed $1M through a committee chaired by its own lobbyist; and FEC pushback over his dual campaign accounts.

##### Cited Findings

Disclosure and trading
- Oct 5, 2026 (Florida Bulldog): Donalds' FD reports for 2021 to 2024 omitted liabilities (student loans growing to $250,000 to $500,000 by 2024, and a $500,000 to $1M CrossCountry mortgage), amended June 4, 2026; an Aug 13, 2026 amendment added an omitted Nuveen fund in Erika's 401(k). Spokesman Gates McGavick: Donalds is "committed to disclosure"; declined to explain the omissions. CLC's Sept 2024 OCE complaint alleged 108 trades in 2022-23 worth up to $1.6M without STOCK Act reports; outcome not public., [Florida Bulldog, Oct 5, 2026](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2026/10/u-s-rep-donalds-significantly-inaccurate-financial-disclosure-forms-entire-time-in-congress/)
- FEC: on Aug 5, 2025 the FEC said his congressional re-election campaign ended when he declared for governor Feb 25, 2025; within two days the House campaign transferred $1.2M to the state PAC; FEC reportedly told him to refund later contributions; his treasurer declined and the account raised $125,000 more through June 2026; on Aug 6, 2026 the treasurer told the FEC he was no longer seeking re-election., [Florida Bulldog, Oct 5, 2026](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2026/10/u-s-rep-donalds-significantly-inaccurate-financial-disclosure-forms-entire-time-in-congress/)
- April 10, 2025: he and his wife traded $8,008 to $120,000 in stock (Visa, Nu Holdings, Amphenol, Regeneron) a week after "Liberation Day.", [NOTUS via Washington Sun](https://www.washingtonsun.com/congress/byron-donalds-crypto-rules-bitcoin?redirected=notus_org)

Charter schools and Erika Donalds
- Optima Foundation (formed late 2017; renamed Education Freedom Foundation March 2025) received $4.3M from Treasure Coast Classical, $2.4M from Jacksonville Classical schools and $8.4M from Naples Classical; Optima paid Erika $183,326 in 2021, income Donalds did not report in 2021 or 2022. Educator Solutions (fictitious name of ESI Technical, founded by state Rep. John Snyder) was paid $6.9M by the schools 2020-22 and paid Erika more than $500,000. In 2023 the for-profit OptimaEd and Optima Management Solutions took $5.84M; Naples Classical's payments rose more than 25% after switching to the for-profit; Optima Classical Academy paid OptimaEd 89% of $3.59M in government funds over 2023-24. Treasure Coast and Jacksonville ended Foundation contracts (auditors found accounting "deficiencies"). Donalds reports Erika owns 81% of OptimaEd but had not disclosed Optima Management Services., [Florida Bulldog, June 29, 2025](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2025/06/firms-belonging-to-rep-donalds-wife-grabbed-millions-in-charter-school-contracts/)
- Aug 2025 amended filings acknowledged the Optima Management Services stake; valued OptimaEd at $1M to $5M (2023) then $500K to $1M (2024); listed her income as "N/A.", [Florida Bulldog, Sept 7, 2025](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2025/09/disclosures-deepend-mystery-rep-donaldss-wifes-charter-school-business/)
- Treasure Coast Classical's suit (filed late 2024, dismissed on venue May 2025, on appeal) alleges an undisclosed conflict with Summit Construction (Jacksonville, co-founder Charles "Chip" Cordes, who gave Donalds $8,400 in 2020 matched by his wife plus $2,000 in 2021), an $11M contract, and a $127,487.98 invoice paid without board approval., [Florida Bulldog, Jan 4, 2026](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2026/01/charter-business-owned-rep-donalds-wife-cut-side-deals-construction-firm-suit-says/)
- Erika: "people should be paid for their services"; alignment with Yass predates meeting him through the Yass Prize (OptimaEd was a 2023 semifinalist); she says she does no business with the state. Campaign: her work is a longstanding career, not a conflict., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-billionaire-trying-to-reinvent-florida-schools-has-a-7-5-million-bet-on-byron-donalds-40565603/); [FlaglerLive](https://flaglerlive.com/gospel-of-school-choice/)

Immigration detention vendors (WUSF, Sept 30, 2026)
- FDEM owed more than $417M on 592 unpaid Operation Vigil Sentry invoices. GEO Group gave Donalds $2.5M (owed nearly $40M via GEO|CDR Baker LLC; also $50,000 to Uthmeier, $15,000 to Ingoglia); ARS/IRG more than $100,000 (owed more than $68M); CDR entities and Carlos Duart more than $620,000 (CDR Health paid $5.18M; still owed more than $40M); Lemoine $59,000 (owed $27.3M); Critical Response Strategies $10,000 (owed more than $35M). None gave to Jolly. Duart: "Florida is our home..." WUSF: the reporting "does not establish whether donations were linked to contract decisions or payments.", [WUSF](https://www.wusf.org/politics-issues/2026-09-30/immigration-detention-money-flows-florida-governors-race). GEO's earlier totals: $500,000 by Jan 2026, $1.5M by July 2026., [News4Jax](https://www.news4jax.com/news/politics/2026/01/15/rep-byron-donalds-continues-to-hold-wide-fundraising-advantage-in-floridas-race-for-governor-here-are-his-top-donors/); [WGCU](https://www.wgcu.org/housing/2026-07-01/airbnb-gives-1-million-to-byron-donalds-as-rentals-remain-a-suncoast-flashpoint)

Gambling and prediction markets
- Through July 2026: Seminole Tribe about $4M to Donalds (about $9M statewide); FanDuel most of $581,000; DK Crown (DraftKings) most of $525,000; Sports Betting Alliance $250,000; Caesars $245,000; Robinhood $200,000; PrizePicks $100,000; Kalshi $75,000; Poarch Creek $100,000; Yass $7.5M plus $3.5M. Steve Wynn $1M (Q1 2026)., [Gambling Insider, Aug 12, 2026](https://www.gamblinginsider.com/news/185823/gambling-prediction-markets-florida-elections-byron-donalds); [CardPlayer, Aug 13, 2026](https://www.cardplayer.com/poker-news/1735617-florida-campaign-donations-signal-possible-gaming-expansion); [Highlands News-Sun](https://www.midfloridanewspapers.com/highlands_news-sun/news/donalds-dominates-fundraising-in-governor-race/article_70925d94-73c0-4f61-9737-e3805c0a1d70.html)
- Stakes: the 2021 compact's exclusivity delivers roughly $1B a year to the state; Kalshi, Polymarket, FanDuel Predicts and DraftKings Predictions operate under CFTC oversight outside the compact; DeSantis said on CNBC (Feb 23, 2026) a prediction market could conflict with the compact; attorney Daniel Wallach calls prediction markets "the next battleground" and the Tribe wants online casino gaming. Donalds has taken no public position; his campaign says donations do not mean he would act for donors; Trump has backed prediction markets., [WOKV, June 17, 2026](https://www.wokv.com/news/local/could-prediction-market-offerings-put-floridas-gaming-compact-with-seminole-tribe-risk/N32U5WRRGJE4XLDEW4EILHUFSY/); [CDC Gaming](https://cdcgaming.com/brief/florida-governor-raises-questions-about-kalshi-crowding-seminole-tribe-gambling-rights/); [CardPlayer](https://www.cardplayer.com/poker-news/1735617-florida-campaign-donations-signal-possible-gaming-expansion)
- Kalshi, a donor, ran a market showing Donalds above 95% to win the primary, which he then promoted on social media., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-tiktok-billionaire-bankrolling-byron-donalds-40558946/)

Sugar and Everglades
- Big Sugar did not give directly as of March 2026 per VoteWater, but AIF PAC ($525,000) and The Florida PC ($237,000) did; Mosaic (phosphate) $200,000; nearly $3M from real estate, construction and housing finance. VoteWater says his voting record does not back his "complete Everglades Restoration" rhetoric (citing the LCV scorecard, no specific votes)., [VoteWater, Mar 26, 2026](https://votewater.org/dirty-money-talks-what-does-it-say-about-byron-donalds/)
- Aug 3, 2026: Florida Crystals gave $500,000 on June 15 to "The Committee" (raised nearly $3.3M since Jan 2025), which gave $360,000 in three contributions to Friends of Byron Donalds; VoteWater says U.S. Sugar did give the Donalds PAC directly (amount not stated)., [VoteWater, Aug 3, 2026](https://votewater.org/the-pac-to-pac-shell-game-how-a-dollar-bill-gets-from-big-sugar-to-your-legislator/)
- Jason Garcia (Seeking Rents) posted on X that recent big donors to Donalds included Florida Crystals ($205,000), the DeVos family ($1M), Disney (about $78,000 in-kind), and NE Florida developers Davis family/DDI, PARC Group and Hutson Cos. ($450,000). The post could not be fetched directly; the text is from the search index and the date is not confirmed, so treat as provisional pending the Oct 30 disclosure., [Jason Garcia on X](https://x.com/Jason_Garcia/status/2103557195010859407)
- Sugar's federal stake: the One Big Beautiful Bill (which Donalds voted for July 3, 2025) raised the sugar loan rate from 19.75 to 24 cents per pound; Florida Crystals gave $1M to MAGA Inc. in 2024 and $2M per a Sept 2025 filing., [Florida Phoenix, June 5, 2025](https://floridaphoenix.com/2025/06/05/the-one-big-beautiful-bill-contains-an-ugly-favor-for-floridas-sugar-industry/); [Florida Bulldog, Sept 28, 2025](https://www.floridabulldog.org/2025/09/here-floridians-who-paid-megabucks-to-dine-with-president-donald-trump/)
- Everglades Trust endorsed him Oct 6, 2026 (see KQ2); sugar companies' EAA reservoir suit against the Army Corps is pending per Paradise Progressive and Captains for Clean Water. No Donalds statement on the suit found.

Development and growth
- Airbnb's $1M routed May 19, 2026 through a committee chaired by its lobbyist (see KQ3)., [WGCU](https://www.wgcu.org/housing/2026-07-01/airbnb-gives-1-million-to-byron-donalds-as-rentals-remain-a-suncoast-flashpoint)
- Stephen Ross (Related Companies) and MasTec (Jorge Mas) are named among data center donors., [Seeking Rents, Oct 15, 2026](https://jasongarcia.substack.com/p/data-center-interests-have-put-30)

Jolly's framing and Donalds' answer
- Jolly, Aug 18, 2026: "Byron Donalds has sold the governor's mansion to the highest bidder"; Oct 2026 ad: "Byron Donalds lies for the special interests that control him.", [CBS12](https://cbs12.com/news/florida/david-jolly-hosts-election-night-watch-party-in-miami-lakes-wpec-florida-news-florida-elections-byron-donalds-miami-lakes-david-jolly); [CBS Miami, Oct 6, 2026](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/byron-donalds-new-ads-florida-governor-race-david-jolly/)
- Donalds: "Donors do not set my agenda, the people of Florida do.", [FlaglerLive](https://flaglerlive.com/gospel-of-school-choice/)
- Lincoln Project-affiliated Conservatives for Ethical Leadership (chair J.C. Planas, funded by Affordable Florida) is running anti-Donalds ads in the 17 counties he lost., [Florida Phoenix, Oct 6, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/10/06/new-ad-targets-byron-donalds-in-the-counties-he-lost-in-the-gop-primary/)

##### Inferences
- The strongest "act after money" sequences on record are the GEO lobbying on his own detention bills in 2024 followed by GEO's $2.5M, and the Yass-funded school choice ecosystem around Erika Donalds' businesses. The bitcoin episode is the only one with a same-day official act.

##### Gaps
- No earmark or letter-of-support-for-donor story found; no Tampa Bay Times or Seeking Rents piece specifically on insurance industry events with Donalds. The Seeking Rents Sept 12 "insurance brokerage led by a billionaire megadonor" item does not name Donalds.
- Tampa Bay Times Oct 7, 2026 piece on the Donaldses' charter wealth could not be fetched.
- State disclosure gap: parties have not disclosed donors since Aug 14; full general election reports due Oct 30, 2026, so late October donor totals (DeVos, Florida Crystals direct) are unverified.

#### Key Question 6: The Israel angle

##### Takeaway
Donalds has a conventional pro-Israel Republican record (Iron Dome 2021, Israel supplemental April 2024, anti-BDS, pro-Israel resolutions) with two notable no votes framed on constitutional grounds (Antisemitism Awareness Act, Combating International Islamophobia Act). Pro-Israel PAC money to his House account is modest ($60K to $105K depending on tracker). The 2026 primary made Israel a wedge because rival James Fishback called Netanyahu a "war criminal"; Donalds answered "Israel is our ally. That is not going to change." No reporting found tying him to Israeli entities.

##### Cited Findings
- Votes (VoteLog roll call list): Yea on H.R. 5323 Iron Dome Supplemental (2021); Yea on H.R. 6126, H.R. 7217 and H.R. 8034 Israel Security Supplemental bills (2023-24); Yea on H.R. 5179 Anti-BDS Labeling Act; Yea on H.Res. 771 standing with Israel, H.Res. 888, H.Con.Res. 57, H.Res. 559 (Iran); Yea on H.R. 340 Hamas financing bill, H.R. 6679; Yea on H.R. 23 Illegitimate Court Counteraction Act (2025); Yea on censuring Rep. Tlaib; Nay on H.R. 6090 Antisemitism Awareness Act (2024); Nay on H.R. 5665 Combating International Islamophobia Act; Nay on several omnibus and NDAA packages., [VoteLog](https://votelog.org/members/fl/byron-donalds). April 20, 2024 Israel aid Yea confirmed by the House Clerk., [House Clerk RC 152](https://clerk.house.gov/Votes/2024152)
- Antisemitism Awareness Act, May 2, 2024 statement: "I hold deep concerns regarding the constitutionality of the anti-Semitism bill"; said it was rushed without amendments and he would support it with amendments modeled on Florida's 2019 H.B. 741., [Donalds statement PDF](https://donalds.house.gov/UploadedFiles/DonaldsSt25202482016.pdf)
- Pro-Israel money: VoteLog counts $104,719 supporting Donalds 2020 to 2026 ($18,500 PACs, $50,600 from seven individuals, $45,119 bundled via 41 conduit donors), $0 opposing., [VoteLog](https://votelog.org/members/fl/byron-donalds). WhoFundsMyRep: $74,692 total pro-Israel PAC money, of which AIPAC PAC $59,864, RJC PAC $5,827, American Principles $5,500, US Israel PAC $3,500; latest transaction Mar 31, 2025; no independent expenditures on record., [WhoFundsMyRep](https://whofundsmyrep.com/rep/byron-donalds). FundVoter shows AIPAC as his top 2026-cycle House contributor ($31,238 net or $59,864 depending on the list)., [FundVoter](https://fundvoter.org/politicians/byron-donalds). Track AIPAC claimed "$84,000 and counting" in a July 2024 post., [Track AIPAC on X](https://x.com/TrackAIPAC/status/1815916931133890962). These are third-party aggregations of FEC data with inconsistent methods.
- May 2026 (leaked campaign-event footage): "Israel is our ally. That is not going to change"; anyone seeking to "sever the relationship" serves Iran, China and Russia; opposes divesting Florida from Israeli bonds. Fishback had called for divesting state funds from Israel bonds and called Netanyahu a "war criminal.", [The Floridian, May 8, 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/05/byron-donalds-doubles-down-on-support-for-israel-sides-with-trump-and-rubio/)
- April 2025 town hall: "We should stand behind Israel 100%." Rabbis' letter (Oct 16, 2026) cited his support during the 2021 Hamas rocket attacks., [Hoodline, Oct 16, 2026](https://hoodline.com/2026/10/40-florida-rabbis-back-byron-donalds-for-governor-weeks-before-election/)
- Oct 6, 2026: JNS interview on Israel, Iran, 2028 and his pitch to Jewish voters (body not retrievable)., [JNS](https://www.jns.org/news/u-s-news/in-florida-byron-donalds-aims-to-do-the-opposite-of-new-york-california)
- Primary framing: JNS and National Review characterized Fishback (10%) and Dan Bilzerian as anti-Israel candidates defeated Aug 18, 2026., [JNS, Aug 19, 2026](https://www.jns.org/news/u-s-news/florida-republicans-defeat-alt-right-candidates-in-gubernatorial-house-primaries); [National Review, Aug 19, 2026](https://www.nationalreview.com/news/what-the-florida-primary-results-say-about-antisemitism-in-the-gop/)
- RJC's May 14, 2026 newsletter lists Senate and House PAC endorsements but no Donalds governor endorsement., [RJC](https://www.rjchq.org/rjc_e_newsletter_20260514)

##### Inferences
- Israel is a values and coalition signal in this race rather than a money story; the pro-Israel dollar amounts are small relative to a $149M operation.

##### Gaps
- No reporting found on Donalds visiting Israel, on a formal AIPAC or RJC endorsement for governor, or on any ties to Israeli companies or government entities. JNS and National Review article bodies were not retrievable.

### Note: yass_network_and_kohelet.md

_Folder: Byron Donalds money and donor network_

### Jeff Yass: political network, Kohelet Policy Forum, and money to Byron Donalds

Research date: 2026-10-22. All amounts are as reported by the cited outlet on the cited date. Where a source could not be fetched directly (Bloomberg, NYT, Washington Post, OpenSecrets, FEC, Florida Division of Elections), that is flagged in Gaps and the figure is attributed to the secondary outlet that cited it.

#### Key Question 1: Who is Jeff Yass (key facts)

##### Takeaway
Yass co-founded Susquehanna International Group (SIG) in 1987, lives in Haverford, Lower Merion Township, Pennsylvania, and his fortune (Forbes: $27.6B in April 2024, $77.8B by September 2026) is heavily tied to an early ByteDance (TikTok parent) stake. He is the largest individual donor to Republican outside groups in the 2024 and 2026 cycles.

##### Cited Findings
- SIG co-founded 1987 by Yass with five fellow Binghamton students; described as the largest trader of liquid stocks in the US. Residence: Haverford, Lower Merion Township, PA. Forbes net worth $77.8B as of September 2026, up from $27.6B in April 2024., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass)
- Forbes estimated Yass's net worth at about $27B (April 2024); SIG owns a 15% stake in ByteDance., [CNBC, April 9, 2024](https://www.cnbc.com/2024/04/09/jeff-yass-millions-to-influence-schools-courts-and-markets.html)
- Yass's personal ByteDance stake is about 7%, roughly $21B, per the Wall Street Journal., [Axios, March 21, 2024](https://www.axios.com/2024/03/21/tiktok-jeff-yass-bytedance-donor-conservative)
- By 2020 the Susquehanna Foundation (founded April 1994, Bala Cynwyd PA) owned at least 15% of ByteDance, a stake estimated at up to $15B (citing WSJ)., [InfluenceWatch](https://www.influencewatch.org/person/jeffrey-yass/)
- SIG co-founders Yass, Arthur Dantchik and Joel Greenberg; HQ Bala Cynwyd, PA; 1,500+ employees; offices in US, Ireland, Asia. Yass and Dantchik met in high school and bet horses in Las Vegas together (won $764,000 in 1985)., [Ynet, Aug 7, 2023](https://www.ynetnews.com/business/article/s1ls7upjn)
- Trump met Yass on March 1, 2024 at a Club for Growth donor retreat in Palm Beach, FL; the following week Trump reversed his position on banning TikTok. Club for Growth hired Kellyanne Conway to defend TikTok (Politico, March 9, 2024)., [ABC News, March 11, 2024](https://abcnews.com/Politics/trumps-tiktok-ban-reversal-after-meeting-megadonor-stake/story?id=108013785)
- WSJ (September 2023) reported Yass was a major donor to politicians opposing TikTok restrictions. SIG also bought a substantial share of DWAC (which merged with Trump Media in 2024); the Philadelphia Inquirer reported SIG as market-maker shorted the stock and held no economic position but profited from trading., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass)

##### Inferences
- Yass's TikTok exposure and the timing of Trump's reversal is widely reported as circumstantial; no outlet fetched here documents what was discussed at the March 1, 2024 meeting.

##### Gaps
- Forbes real-time net worth page not fetched; the $77.8B figure comes via Wikipedia's citation of Forbes.

#### Key Question 2: Main political vehicles and totals by cycle

##### Takeaway
Yass's giving runs mainly through Club for Growth Action, Protect Freedom PAC, School Freedom Fund, Pennsylvania school choice PACs (Students First PA, Commonwealth Leaders Fund, Commonwealth Children's Choice Fund), and since 2025 MAGA Inc. Cycle totals reported: ~$25.3M (2020), ~$54M (2022), ~$100M (2024), and $93.8M to $110M (2026 as of Aug-Oct 2026).

##### Cited Findings
Cycle totals
- 2016: just over $5M (Yass and wife). 2018: $3.8M to Club for Growth. 2020: $25.3M, all to Republicans, among the ten largest US donors. 2022: at least $18M in primary spending including Bill McSwain's PA governor bid. 2024: about $100M to Republican groups plus $1M to Moderate PAC, including $6M via Commonwealth Leaders Fund for Dave Sunday (PA AG). 2025: $16M to MAGA Inc. in H1 2025 and $10M to Vivek Ramaswamy's gubernatorial super PAC. 2026: Yass and wife over $89.3M including $20M to Ramaswamy's super PAC, $16M to MAGA Inc., $15M to Club for Growth Action., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass)
- 2022 cycle: about $54M from Jeff and Janine Yass to campaigns and super PACs. 2024 cycle: roughly $100M (citing NYT). 2026 cycle: about $55M as of March 2026 (citing NYT). Lifetime "over $32 million" to Club for Growth; sole funder of School Freedom Fund (~$15M); $2.5M to Protect Freedom PAC., [InfluenceWatch](https://www.influencewatch.org/person/jeffrey-yass/)
- As of March 21, 2024: more than $46M to outside spending groups in the 2024 cycle, the biggest donor, $11M+ ahead of the second-ranked donor (OpenSecrets). Recipients: Club for Growth $16M, Congressional Leadership Fund $10M, Protect Freedom PAC $6M, plus PACs supporting Ramaswamy and Tim Scott., [Axios, March 21, 2024](https://www.axios.com/2024/03/21/tiktok-jeff-yass-bytedance-donor-conservative)
- 2023-24 cycle: more than $100M to super PACs (OpenSecrets, as cited)., [Kentucky Lantern, Dec 9, 2025](https://kentuckylantern.com/2025/12/09/thomas-massie-facing-trumps-opposition-gets-a-hand-from-billionaire-megadonor/)
- 2026 cycle: $93.8M to super PACs supporting Republicans, naming MAGA Inc. and School Freedom Fund (citing NYT analysis dated Aug 28, 2026)., [Daily Caller, Aug 31, 2026](https://dailycaller.com/2026/08/31/20-megadonors-2026-midterm-elections/)
- 2026 cycle: Jeff and Janine Yass $110M for the GOP, one of four donors over $100M, listed after Musk and the Uihleins (citing FEC filings)., [Spectrum News, Oct 16, 2026](https://spectrumlocalnews.com/us/snplus/politics/2026/10/16/midterm-elections-top-50-donors-decision-2026)
- Bloomberg headline Oct 22, 2026: "Billionaire Jeff Yass Pours $110 Million Into Republican Midterm Push" (article body not retrievable)., [Bloomberg, Oct 22, 2026](https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-10-22/billionaire-jeff-yass-pours-110-million-into-republican-midterm-push)

Club for Growth / Club for Growth Action
- Yass has given about $61M to Club for Growth since 2010 (WSJ figures); his contributions are 24% of the PAC's political-spending arm. Club for Growth opposes TikTok bans; McIntosh says its involvement dates to 2022., [Philadelphia Inquirer, Sept 20, 2023](https://www.inquirer.com/business/jeffrey-yass-club-for-growth-pac-20230920.html)
- Yass gave more than $15M to Club for Growth's super PAC months before the group backed Trump (2024)., [CNBC, April 9, 2024](https://www.cnbc.com/2024/04/09/jeff-yass-millions-to-influence-schools-courts-and-markets.html)
- Club for Growth Action: Yass is the main source of its 2026 money (FEC data); raised more than $50M as of Aug 31, 2026; nearly $5M in independent expenditures; $4.5M+ transferred to Win It Back PAC; 2024 cycle spending ~$89.8M; McIntosh told Fox in March 2026 the group aimed to raise and spend $175M this cycle., [FactCheck.org, Oct 2026](https://www.factcheck.org/2026/10/club-for-growth-action-8/)
- Yass supplied 30% of Club for Growth Super PAC funding and 65% of Protect Freedom PAC funding in the 2020 cycle (citing OpenSecrets)., [J Street, Jan 2023](https://jstreet.org/shushan-street-meet-the-israeli-organization-behind-the-legislation-to-subvert-israeli-democracy-and-the-american-billionaires-that-fund-it/)

Protect Freedom PAC
- Yass gave $7.5M on Oct 20 (2025) when the PAC had ~$114,000 cash on hand; $1M on Oct 23 to pro-Massie Kentucky First PAC. Yass has provided about 95% of Protect Freedom PAC's money (~$35M) over the past five years., [Kentucky Lantern, Dec 9, 2025](https://kentuckylantern.com/2025/12/09/thomas-massie-facing-trumps-opposition-gets-a-hand-from-billionaire-megadonor/)
- Substack claim: $8M to Protect Freedom PAC in 2024; $39.5M+ to Club for Growth Action across 2023-2024; $11M+ to School Freedom Fund; $3.5M to AFC Victory Fund (unverified opinion newsletter, no filings cited)., [Steve Edward Farley Substack](https://steveedwardfarley.substack.com/p/how-the-donalds-network-the-school)

School Freedom Fund
- $5M in November 2021 for ads supporting Republicans in the 2022 cycle., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass)

Pennsylvania school choice PACs
- Students First PA: Yass contributed $41.7M from 2010 to 2022 and co-founded the PAC. Commonwealth Leaders Fund: $6M in 2024 PA AG race., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass)
- May 2021: Students First PAC (Yass's own PAC) took $10.5M over six months and transferred it all to the Commonwealth Children's Choice Fund, which then held ~$11.5M almost all from Yass; Students First gave $8.5M to a Matt Brouillette PAC in November 2020; Children's Choice Fund sent $1.9M to Commonwealth Leaders Fund before the 2020 election., [WHYY, May 12, 2021](https://whyy.org/articles/jeff-yass-the-richest-man-in-pa-is-single-handedly-keeping-school-choice-pacs-flush)
- Yass has spent more than $75M on Pennsylvania elections since 2017., [WUSF, Sept 9, 2026](https://wusf.org/text/politics-issues/2026-09-09/what-to-know-about-pacs-your-florida-breaks-it-down)
- 2022: a Yass-backed PAC spent $12.9M across several PACs for Bill McSwain's governor run and funded winning primary challengers to Stan Saylor and Pat Browne. Yass quotes on school choice: "I've never been more sure about any bet I've ever made"; calls it "a civil rights issue." Yass Prize founded ~2021 with wife Janine., [Philadelphia Magazine, Aug 24, 2024](https://www.phillymag.com/news/2024/08/24/jeff-yass-school-choice)

Trump-related giving
- $16M to MAGA Inc. in H1 2025 while Trump postponed the legally mandated TikTok ban., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-tiktok-billionaire-bankrolling-byron-donalds-40558946/)

Other / foundations
- Yass-led foundations gave over $25M to libertarian nonprofits since 2016 (Cato ~$6.8M from Susquehanna and Claws foundations, Institute for Justice $5M+). Susquehanna Foundation received $250M+ in 2020 from Susquehanna Growth Equity Fund III. Yass is the only known contributor to Moderate PAC., [CNBC, April 9, 2024](https://www.cnbc.com/2024/04/09/jeff-yass-millions-to-influence-schools-courts-and-markets.html)
- Moderate PAC (2023): Yass its only contributor; pledged to raise $20M to defeat progressive Democratic primary challengers., [The Intercept, Feb 16, 2023](https://theintercept.com/2023/02/16/israel-jeff-yass-kohelet)
- Guardian/Responsible Statecraft investigation: Yass-directed Claws Foundation gave $16M to pro-Israel and anti-Muslim groups, including $7.9M to Jerusalem Online University (2014-2019), $250,000 to David Horowitz Freedom Center (2012-2013), $35,000 to Center for Security Policy., [Daily Beast summarizing Guardian](https://blogs.thedailybeast.com/gop-megadonor-jeff-yass-poured-dollar16m-into-anti-muslim-groups-report)

##### Inferences
- Reported cycle totals vary by source and date because they count different things (super PAC only vs. all federal vs. including state). The $110M 2026 figure (Spectrum/Bloomberg, Oct 2026) is the most recent.

##### Gaps
- OpenSecrets donor page and FEC site were blocked; totals above are as relayed by Axios, Kentucky Lantern, Daily Caller, Spectrum, Wikipedia. Exact per-committee 2026 breakdown from Bloomberg not retrievable.
- Commonwealth Leaders Fund lifetime total from Yass not found in a single source.

#### Key Question 3: Kohelet Policy Forum, Yass and Dantchik

##### Takeaway
Kohelet (founded January 2012 by Moshe Koppel) produced the policy papers underpinning the 2023 judicial overhaul pushed by Yariv Levin and Simcha Rothman. Haaretz (March 2021) named both Yass and Arthur Dantchik as Kohelet's principal funders through opaque US vehicles, but later reporting (NYT, Washington Post, Calcalist, JTA) says Yass never donated and Dantchik was the dominant donor (Calcalist: ~93% of budget). Dantchik announced on August 4, 2023 that he had stopped funding Israeli think tanks including Kohelet; Kohelet cut roughly half its staff in April 2024 and its donations fell from 30M shekels (2022) to 13.6M shekels (2024).

##### Cited Findings
What Kohelet is
- Established January 2012 by Moshe Koppel (chair), run with Avraham Diskin, Avi Bell and Eugene Kontorovich. Says it takes only private donations. Largest donations are anonymous, several million dollars, routed through the American nonprofit American Friends of Kohelet Policy Forum (Haaretz, Oct 5, 2018)., [Wikipedia: Kohelet Policy Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohelet_Policy_Forum)
- Koppel reportedly wrote the original draft of the 2018 Basic Law: Nation-State; Kohelet provided legal arguments for Pompeo's 2019 settlements announcement; works closely with the US-based Tikvah Fund. Koppel's account: he met one of the two donors (he won't say which) about an investment algorithm, found shared political vision, submitted a budget, and funds arrived within days. Note: J Street is an advocacy organization., [J Street, Jan 2023](https://jstreet.org/shushan-street-meet-the-israeli-organization-behind-the-legislation-to-subvert-israeli-democracy-and-the-american-billionaires-that-fund-it/)

Role in the 2023 judicial overhaul
- Kohelet policy papers underpinned the attempted 2023 reform: government control of judicial appointments, limits on judicial review, an override clause allowing 61 of 120 MKs to overrule the Supreme Court, limits on legal advisers' authority. On March 14, 2023 Koppel suggested dropping the override clause. Kohelet's Michael Sarel publicly opposed the reform in a personal paper; economist Tom Sadeh resigned., [Wikipedia: Kohelet Policy Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohelet_Policy_Forum)
- Kohelet reportedly wrote the legislation submitted by Justice Minister Yariv Levin and Simcha Rothman (chair, Knesset Constitution, Law and Justice Committee)., [J Street, Jan 2023](https://jstreet.org/shushan-street-meet-the-israeli-organization-behind-the-legislation-to-subvert-israeli-democracy-and-the-american-billionaires-that-fund-it/)
- AP: Kohelet is "one of the main architects" of the overhaul; Israeli media reported it took part in negotiations; a Likud member said earlier in 2023 the think tank gave the government the same proposal presented to the Knesset. First major overhaul measure passed the Knesset in July 2023., [PBS/AP, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/world/an-american-billionaire-says-hell-stop-funding-the-think-tank-behind-israels-controversial-judicial-overhaul)
- Calcalist: Kohelet's work influences ministers Yariv Levin and Bezalel Smotrich., [Calcalist, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.calcalistech.com/ctechnews/article/hkora4ci3)
- Channel 12 (Aug 7, 2023): after the funding cutoff, senior Kohelet members met coalition ministers and MKs to urge a pause in the legislation., [Wikipedia: Kohelet Policy Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohelet_Policy_Forum)

Who funded Kohelet: Yass vs Dantchik (confirmed vs alleged)
- ALLEGED (Haaretz, March 2021): Haaretz reported that Yass and Dantchik were behind a large portion of Kohelet donations., [Wikipedia: Jeff Yass](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeff_Yass); Haaretz's 2021 investigation found a network of opaque US third-party groups through which Dantchik and others channeled donations and identified Dantchik as one of two principal supporters, [PBS/AP, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/world/an-american-billionaire-says-hell-stop-funding-the-think-tank-behind-israels-controversial-judicial-overhaul)
- The Intercept (Feb 16, 2023, Akela Lacy) called Yass "a key benefactor" of Kohelet, citing Haaretz; gave no amounts; Yass did not respond., [The Intercept](https://theintercept.com/2023/02/16/israel-jeff-yass-kohelet)
- DISPUTED/DENIED: Calcalist (Aug 4, 2023), citing sources close to Dantchik, reported that Yass "never donated" to Kohelet., [Calcalist](https://www.calcalistech.com/ctechnews/article/hkora4ci3). JTA says this "confirms New York Times and Washington Post reporting and contradicts the 2021 Haaretz investigation.", [JTA, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.jta.org/2023/08/04/israel/wall-street-billionaire-ends-funding-for-think-tank-behind-netanyahus-judicial-overhaul-plan)
- InfluenceWatch summarizing the Washington Post (March 2023): an anonymous source close to the men told WaPo that Dantchik is not the sole funder but that Yass never donated to Kohelet; as of March 2023 neither had confirmed being a donor., [InfluenceWatch](https://www.influencewatch.org/person/jeffrey-yass/)
- Wikipedia: "Acquaintances of Yass dispute this and say he has never donated to Kohelet." Calcalist reported Dantchik's donations may account for about 93% of Kohelet's budget., [Wikipedia: Kohelet Policy Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohelet_Policy_Forum)
- May 21, 2021: Jewish Voice for Peace staged a sit-in at SIG's Bala Cynwyd lobby demanding Yass and Dantchik stop funding Kohelet; SIG declined comment; neither man responded., [WHYY, May 21, 2021](https://whyy.org/articles/jewish-activists-protest-bala-cynwyd-billionaires-support-of-right-wing-israeli-think-tank/)

The funding vehicle and amounts (Dantchik)
- Shomrim/TheMarker investigation (Aug 22, 2023): Dantchik's Claws Foundation (founded 2004; funded solely by Dantchik; day-to-day run by Sterling Foundation Management) sent about $12M in 2021 to Silver Valley Research Foundation, a US nonprofit set up months earlier describing itself as focused on "medical research"; Silver Valley passed most of it to the Central Fund of Israel (CFI), which was Kohelet's sole donor in its 2021 Israeli filings. Kohelet received ~25M shekels (~$7.7M) from CFI in 2021 and ~29M shekels (~$8.5M) in 2022. CFI director Jay Marcus said the fund does everything "by the book." Dantchik gave about $220M to Claws 2013-2020 (~$110M in 2020 alone). Kohelet and Dantchik declined to respond., [Shomrim, Aug 22, 2023](https://www.shomrim.news/eng/danchik-kohelet)
- The exact amount Dantchik gave Kohelet has not been disclosed; Kohelet is not required to disclose donors. Kohelet said its donations "are broad-based and increasing steadily.", [PBS/AP, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.pbs.org/newshour/world/an-american-billionaire-says-hell-stop-funding-the-think-tank-behind-israels-controversial-judicial-overhaul)
- Dantchik's Claws Foundation gave nearly $3.3M to the Shalom Hartman Institute in 2021 (a separate, pluralist group). Dantchik is on the board of the Institute for Justice., [JTA, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.jta.org/2023/08/04/israel/wall-street-billionaire-ends-funding-for-think-tank-behind-netanyahus-judicial-overhaul-plan); [Ynet, Aug 7, 2023](https://www.ynetnews.com/business/article/s1ls7upjn)
- Dantchik is a ByteDance board member and linked to at least 13 Israeli high-tech investments; Forbes ~$7.5B., [Calcalist, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.calcalistech.com/ctechnews/article/hkora4ci3)

The 2023 withdrawal
- August 4, 2023: Calcalist exclusive that Dantchik, Kohelet's main donor, stopped supporting it. Statement: "I stopped donating to think tanks in Israel, including the Kohelet Policy Forum"; Israel should "focus on healing and national unity.", [Calcalist, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.calcalistech.com/ctechnews/article/hkora4ci3); [JTA, Aug 4, 2023](https://www.jta.org/2023/08/04/israel/wall-street-billionaire-ends-funding-for-think-tank-behind-netanyahus-judicial-overhaul-plan)
- Timing of when he actually stopped is "unclear." Protesters had demonstrated at his Philadelphia-area home, office and synagogue for about thirty weeks and ran a newspaper ad., [JTA](https://www.jta.org/2023/08/04/israel/wall-street-billionaire-ends-funding-for-think-tank-behind-netanyahus-judicial-overhaul-plan); [Jewish Press, Aug 6, 2023 (opinion-leaning, sympathetic to Kohelet)](https://www.jewishpress.com/news/left-vs-right/kohelets-billionaire-donor-gives-in-after-months-of-incessant-harassment-by-anarchists/2023/08/06)
- No source fetched reports Yass "withdrawing" funding, because the better-sourced reporting holds that he never gave. The "Yass and Dantchik withdrew" framing conflates the two.

Kohelet's later financial troubles
- April 2, 2024: Kohelet announced it would cut about half its staff because donations dried up (Calcalist and Haaretz); overlap with the Misgav Institute makes it hard to tell whether the cuts are partly a rebrand., [Wikipedia: Kohelet Policy Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohelet_Policy_Forum); Israel Hayom: "parting ways with dozens of staff members" after Dantchik stopped funding the previous summer., [Israel Hayom, April 2, 2024](https://www.israelhayom.com/2024/04/02/kohelet-policy-forum-scales-down-activity-due-to-funding-crunch/)
- Kohelet donations: 30M shekels (~$8.9M) in 2022, 24M shekels (~$7M) in 2023, 13.6M shekels (~$4M) in 2024, a 43.2% drop. Payroll fell 44% (14.6M to 8.2M shekels); research spend fell 52.5% (9.2M to 4.4M shekels); dozens fired; Kohelet stopped supporting other right-wing NGOs. In 2024 CFI supplied ~$3.5M of the ~$4M; "Friends of Kohelet" (US) supplied the rest; a crowdfunding drive "Davka Kohelet" (launched Aug 2024) raised 4.1M+ shekels (~$1.2M). Spokesperson declined to say whether there is a new chief donor., [Shomrim](https://www.shomrim.news/eng/funding-crisis-forces-kohelet-to-cut-salaries-research-and-support-for-other-groups)
- Feb 2025: Knesset set to revisit Kohelet's tax benefits (headline only)., [Israel Hayom, Feb 9, 2025](https://www.israelhayom.com/2025/02/09/knesset-set-to-revisit-tax-benefits-for-the-kohelet-policy-forum/)

##### Inferences
- Reporting consensus as of 2023-2024: Dantchik, not Yass, was the money behind Kohelet; the Yass link rests on the 2021 Haaretz investigation and was subsequently contradicted by NYT, WaPo, Calcalist and sources close to both men. Any profile should state that Yass's funding of Kohelet is alleged by Haaretz and denied by people close to him, while Dantchik's is confirmed by his own statement.
- Yass remains tied to Kohelet-adjacent networks indirectly: Claws Foundation (where he served as a director) grants to pro-Israel groups per the Guardian; Eugene Kontorovich (Kohelet) also appears in the Moderate PAC story.

##### Gaps
- Original Haaretz March 2021 investigation, NYT March 20, 2023 article, and Washington Post March 2023 article were not fetchable (paywalled/blocked); their content is relayed via JTA, InfluenceWatch, Wikipedia and PBS/AP.
- No filing found showing a direct Yass or Susquehanna Foundation grant to American Friends of Kohelet, CFI or Silver Valley.
- The 93% figure is attributed to Calcalist by Wikipedia; the Calcalist English article fetched does not itself state a percentage.

#### Key Question 4: Yass money to Byron Donalds (House races and 2026 governor race)

##### Takeaway
Yass is Donalds' single largest donor in the 2026 governor race: $7.5M directly to the state PAC Friends of Byron Donalds (first $5M by March 2025, reaching $7.5M by mid-2026) plus $3.5M to the super PAC Club for Growth Action Florida, which in turn gave Friends of Byron Donalds $3.75M, for roughly $11M attributed to Yass. Club for Growth PAC, whose largest donor is Yass, spent $2.5M to help Donalds win his 2020 House primary and endorsed him for governor on March 11, 2025.

##### Cited Findings
Governor race (state committee Friends of Byron Donalds PAC, formed Feb 2025)
- Through March 31, 2025: Jeff Yass $5M and Richard Uihlein $1M to Friends of Byron Donalds PAC., [News4Jax, April 14, 2025](https://www.news4jax.com/news/local/2025/04/14/byron-donalds-secures-6-million-from-influential-gop-donors-in-early-fundraising-for-florida-governors-race/)
- Yass gave $5M to Friends of Byron Donalds "in March" (2025); the committee had raised ~$22.6M since forming in February and held $21.1M cash as of Aug 27, 2025., [Florida Politics (Seminole Tribe article)](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/753415-seminole-tribe-antes-up-with-byron-donalds/)
- Yass $5,000,000 to Donalds' PAC (April 18, 2025 article)., [NOTUS/Washington Sun, April 18, 2025](https://www.washingtonsun.com/florida/byron-donalds-governor-race-casey-desantis?redirected=notus_org)
- Yass total $7.5M to Friends of Byron Donalds PAC, the largest single donor, more than double the next. Other top donors: Club for Growth PAC $3.65M, Seminole Tribe $3M, Uihlein $2M, GEO Group $1.5M, Peterffy $1.2M, Wynn $1M, NextEra $1M. Campaign total $81M+; PAC $71M+. Yass also gave $16M to MAGA Inc. in H1 2025., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-tiktok-billionaire-bankrolling-byron-donalds-40558946/)
- As of Sept 7, 2026: Friends of Byron Donalds raised over $100M, the largest affiliated PAC in Florida; Ken Griffin made its largest single donation (amount not given); Yass $7.5M direct plus $3.5M to super PAC Club for Growth Action Florida, which gave Friends of Byron Donalds $3.75M; WUSF totals Yass at "$11 million." Yass and Donalds' campaign did not respond., [WUSF, Sept 9, 2026](https://wusf.org/text/politics-issues/2026-09-09/what-to-know-about-pacs-your-florida-breaks-it-down)
- Tampa Bay Times analysis (fundraising as of Sept 18, 2026): 17 billionaires gave Donalds $23.3M (18% of his total); Yass about $11M, comprising $7.5M direct and $3.7M to another committee that passed the same amount to Donalds; Club for Growth Action Florida and GOPAC Election Fund together made up 24% of Donalds' large-dollar funds; DeVos family ~$1M., [Tampa Bay Times via KDH News, Oct 2, 2026](https://kdhnews.com/news/politics/18-billionaires-have-funded-the-florida-governor-race-17-gave-to-donalds/article_f4a68240-1521-537e-b8cb-9006156ebc14.html)
- Friends of Byron Donalds raised $40,804,593.40 from Feb 2025 through Q4 2025; Q4 2025 donors included Seminole Tribe $1M, FanDuel $500K, DK Crown $500K, GEO $500K, NextEra $500K., [News4Jax, Jan 15, 2026](https://www.news4jax.com/news/politics/2026/01/15/rep-byron-donalds-continues-to-hold-wide-fundraising-advantage-in-floridas-race-for-governor-here-are-his-top-donors/)
- Campaign claims $45M raised in 2025, $13.1M in Q4 2025., [Floridian Press, Jan 5, 2026](https://floridianpress.com/2026/01/byron-donalds-hauls-45m-in-2025-over-13m-in-q4-for-gubernatorial-bid/)
- Substack (unverified opinion): Yass $7.5M "spanning 2025 and 2026"; Club for Growth PAC $3.65M; Uihlein $2M in two installments., [Steve Edward Farley Substack](https://steveedwardfarley.substack.com/p/how-the-donalds-network-the-school)

House races and Club for Growth
- Club for Growth says it spent $2.5M to help Donalds win his 2020 congressional primary; Club for Growth PAC endorsed Donalds for governor on Tuesday, March 11, 2025, after Trump's endorsement ("RUN, BYRON, RUN!"). McIntosh: "a proven constitutional conservative.", [Fox News, March 11, 2025](https://www.foxnews.com/politics/first-fox-top-conservative-group-aligns-trump-makes-major-endorsement-high-profile-race.print)
- Club for Growth PAC "proud to have supported Rep. Donalds since his first election to Congress in 2020"; the endorsement came before Casey DeSantis decided whether to run., [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/725626-club-for-growth-pac-doesnt-wait-on-casey-desantis-backing-byron-donalds-early/)
- Club for Growth endorsed Donalds in the FL-19 GOP primary as early as the 2018 cycle (McIntosh: "a constitutional conservative with invaluable experience in finance")., [Florida Politics, 2018](https://floridapolitics.com/?p=339590)
- Club for Growth PAC congratulated Donalds on winning the FL-19 primary (Aug 2020)., [Club for Growth](https://www.clubforgrowth.org/club-for-growth-pac-congratulates-byron-donalds-on-winning-fl-19-republican-primary/)

##### Inferences
- Yass's direct Florida giving appears in at least two tranches: $5M by March 2025 and an additional $2.5M at some point between September 2025 and mid-2026 (the Aug 2025 Florida Politics piece still shows $5M; 2026 reporting shows $7.5M). Exact date of the second tranche is not in any fetched source.
- Yass's 2020 House-primary support for Donalds was indirect, via Club for Growth Action's $2.5M; no direct Yass-to-Donalds federal contribution was found in fetched sources.

##### Gaps
- Florida Division of Elections database query failed (proxy 403) and Transparency USA pages failed; exact contribution dates for the $5M and $2.5M tranches and for the $3.5M to Club for Growth Action Florida are not confirmed from primary records.
- FEC/OpenSecrets individual-contribution lookup for Yass to Byron Donalds for Congress (C00733329) could not be run (robots disallowed). No source found reporting a direct Yass contribution to Donalds' House campaign or leadership PAC.
- Bradenton Times article on out-of-state billionaire cash could not be read.

#### Key Question 5: Policy asks tied to Yass money and Donalds' alignment

##### Takeaway
Yass's stated priority is school choice; his vehicles also push against TikTok restrictions and for low-tax, deregulatory policy. Donalds aligns on school choice (Erika Donalds' Optima charter network; proposals to treat parents as consumers and expand ESAs) but voted YEA on the March 13, 2024 TikTok divest-or-ban bill (H.R. 7521, Roll Call 86, 352-65), against the position of Yass and Club for Growth.

##### Cited Findings
- Donalds voted Yea on H.R. 7521 (TikTok divestiture), Roll Call 86, March 13, 2024; 352 Yea, 65 Nay, 1 Present. FL Nays: Frost, Gaetz, Steube., [House Clerk, Roll Call 86](https://clerk.house.gov/Votes/202486)
- Club for Growth opposes TikTok bans (McIntosh 2023: letting government ban apps is "a huge restriction on phone freedom"); hired Kellyanne Conway to defend TikTok (March 2024)., [ABC News, March 11, 2024](https://abcnews.com/Politics/trumps-tiktok-ban-reversal-after-meeting-megadonor-stake/story?id=108013785); [Inquirer, Sept 20, 2023](https://www.inquirer.com/business/jeffrey-yass-club-for-growth-pac-20230920.html)
- Yass has said school choice is his main political motivation; a Susquehanna spokesperson said "Byron Donalds is a school choice warrior"; Yass praised Donalds as a candidate he was proud to support after Texas's voucher win. Donalds couple proposals: replace A-F school grades with individualized learning plans, expand apprenticeships, embrace AI/ed-tech, increase private-sector participation treating parents as consumers and schools as competitors, use existing voucher programs as a base. Erika Donalds' Optima Foundation/OptimaEd/Optima Management Solutions have received millions in taxpayer-funded contracts (Miami Herald); OptimaEd was a 2023 Yass Prize semifinalist. Florida's 2023 law made every K-12 student eligible for ESAs; 500K+ students on scholarships. Texas passed universal ESAs after Yass-backed primary challenges to anti-voucher Republicans. Erika Donalds: "Profit is not going to be a bad word when it comes to the education industry." Donalds did not respond., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-billionaire-trying-to-reinvent-florida-schools-has-a-7-5-million-bet-on-byron-donalds-40565603/)
- Yass-funded Club for Growth's stated mission: "take on any Member of Congress on policy who fails to uphold basic economic conservative principles"; backs free-market, limited-government conservatives including in GOP primaries., [FactCheck.org, Oct 2026](https://www.factcheck.org/2026/10/club-for-growth-action-8/)
- Club for Growth praised Donalds in 2018 for "cutting taxes.", [Florida Politics, 2018](https://floridapolitics.com/?p=339590)
- Yass-led foundations: $25M+ to libertarian nonprofits (Cato, Institute for Justice) since 2016., [CNBC, April 9, 2024](https://www.cnbc.com/2024/04/09/jeff-yass-millions-to-influence-schools-courts-and-markets.html)
- Yass personal school-choice framing: "the biggest bet I've ever made," "a civil rights issue.", [Philadelphia Magazine, Aug 24, 2024](https://www.phillymag.com/news/2024/08/24/jeff-yass-school-choice)
- Kalshi gave $25,000 to Donalds' political committee (prediction markets interest)., [Miami New Times](https://www.miaminewtimes.com/news/the-tiktok-billionaire-bankrolling-byron-donalds-40558946/)

##### Inferences
- The Yass-Donalds alignment is strongest on school choice (the one issue a Yass spokesperson explicitly cited), not on TikTok, where Donalds' recorded vote ran against Yass's interest. Any claim that Yass money bought Donalds' TikTok position is not supported by the vote record.
- No source fetched documents Yass asks on crypto or capital gains specifically tied to Donalds.

##### Gaps
- No fetched source documents specific Donalds votes on federal school choice bills or a statement from Donalds about Yass. Donalds' campaign did not respond to Miami New Times or WUSF.
- Crypto and capital gains policy linkage between Yass and Donalds not found.

#### Key Question 6: Reporting explicitly linking Yass to the Donalds governor bid

##### Takeaway
Four outlets explicitly tie Yass to the Donalds governor campaign as its largest donor: News4Jax (April 2025), Miami New Times (two pieces, 2026), WUSF (Sept 2026) and the Tampa Bay Times (Oct 2026). Club for Growth PAC's March 11, 2025 endorsement is the formal institutional link; Club for Growth Action Florida is the Yass-funded super PAC conduit.

##### Cited Findings
