- WLRN Sept 21, 2026: Realtors pour millions in as opponents warn of budget cuts, [WLRN, Sept 21, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/light/government-politics/2026-09-21/florida-realtors-pour-millions-of-dollars-into-amendment-3-push-as-opponents-warn-of-budget-cuts)

- Donalds promoted his economic plans Aug 21, 2026, [WLRN, Aug 21, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/light/government-politics/2026-08-21/byron-donalds-promotes-his-economic-plans-for-florida-defends-trumps-record)
- The Taxation and Budget Reform Commission convenes every 20 years; the next is 2027, [Wikipedia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Florida_Taxation_and_Budget_Reform_Commission)

##### Inferences
- The Realtors' return on an $18 million investment is transactional: a bigger homestead exemption raises what buyers can afford to pay, which raises prices and commissions. Benefit is concentrated among higher value homesteads; renters, commercial and non homestead owners get nothing and face the backfill (higher millage on non homestead property, fees like the Sanford ambulance charge, or service cuts). The sheriffs' split (Judd against, Miami-Dade for) tracks whether a county has non homestead tax base to absorb the shift.
- The governor's control is through the TBRC (he appoints 11 of 25 members), the veto over the Legislature's implementing bills and the fiscally constrained county stabilization funds in the budget. Donalds' "sing for supper" line signals he would fund rural county backfill from the state budget, which is an inference about intent, not a costed plan.

#### Key question 5: Insurance. Citizens depopulation, tort reform results, Bring Down the Bill, the PolitiFact check, and the industry's 2027 asks

##### Takeaway
Citizens has shrunk to about 270,000 policies through depopulation and a non renewal push, while S&P projected it would post its largest full year underwriting loss. Donalds and Jolly clashed over insurance plans on Sept 16, 2026; PolitiFact's Sept 21, 2026 check of Donalds' claim that Jolly's plan is a "$1,000 hurricane tax" found the number "comes with a lot of fine print."

##### Cited Findings
- Citizens cut its policy count to 270,000 amid a non renewal push, [Insurance Business](https://www.insurancebusinessmag.com/us/news/property/citizens-cuts-policy-count-to-270000-amid-nonrenewal-push-588731.aspx)
- Citizens shrank to a fraction of its peak as depopulation reshaped the market, [The Florida Press](https://www.thefloridapress.com/news/citizens-property-insurance-shrinks-to-a-fraction-of-its-peak-as-depopulation-re)
- S&P: rapidly shrinking Citizens projects its largest full year underwriting loss, [S&P Global Market Intelligence](https://spglobal.com/market-intelligence/en/news-insights/research/rapidly-shrinking-citizens-property-projects-largest-full-year-underwriting-loss)
- James Madison Institute argued for restoring Citizens to insurer of last resort, [JMI](https://jamesmadison.org/from-backstop-to-market-participant-reassessing-citizens-role-restoring-citizens-property-insurance-corporation-to-its-role-as-floridas-insurer-of-last-resort/)
- Jolly and Donalds clashed on property insurance proposals Sept 16 to 17, 2026, [Florida Phoenix, Sept 16, 2026](https://floridaphoenix.com/2026/09/16/jolly-and-donalds-clash-on-property-insurance-proposals/); [WLRN, Sept 17, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/light/government-politics/2026-09-17/jolly-and-donalds-clash-on-property-insurance-proposals); [InsuranceNewsNet](https://insurancenewsnet.com/oarticle/jolly-and-donalds-clash-on-how-to-fix-floridas-property-insurance-crisis); [Beinsure](https://beinsure.com/news/jolly-donalds-outline-florida-property-insurance-plans/)
- PolitiFact Sept 21, 2026: "$1,000 hurricane tax? Fact-checking claims about Jolly's proposal", [PolitiFact, Sept 21, 2026](https://politifact.com/article/2026/sep/21/jolly-donalds-hurricane-insurance-1000-florida/); [WLRN repost, Sept 23, 2026](https://www.wlrn.org/government-politics/2026-09-23/1-000-hurricane-tax-fact-checking-claims-about-jollys-proposal-for-florida-homeowners-insurance)
- A separate check: "Donalds calls Jolly's insurance plan a hurricane tax. The number comes with a lot of fine print.", [Yahoo / wire](https://www.yahoo.com/news/politics/articles/donalds-calls-jolly-insurance-plan-073819574.html); [Yahoo / wire](https://www.yahoo.com/news/politics/articles/fact-checking-donalds-claim-jollys-160637980.html)
- WUFT and WUSF ran broader fact checks of both candidates Oct 16 to 18, 2026, [WUFT, Oct 16, 2026](https://www.wuft.org/text/politics/2026-10-16/fact-checking-claims-from-florida-governor-candidates-david-jolly-and-byron-donalds); [WUSF, Oct 18, 2026](https://www.wusf.org/politics-issues/2026-10-18/fact-checking-claims-from-florida-governor-candidates-david-jolly-and-byron-donalds)
- Folio Weekly Oct 21, 2026 checked the attack ads, [Folio Weekly, Oct 21, 2026](https://folioweekly.com/2026/10/21/fact-or-fiction-floridas-political-attack-ads-are-everywhere-we-checked-the-claims/)

##### Inferences
- "Hurricane tax" is Donalds' label for Jolly's state catastrophe fund or reinsurance proposal that would be funded by assessments; the fact checks say the $1,000 figure depends on assumptions. Donalds' plan keeps the 2022 to 2023 tort reform intact and leans on depopulation and private market entry, which is exactly what Heritage, AFP Action and the Kompothecras family (round one) want preserved: no reopening of one way attorney fees or assignment of benefits, and a Citizens that keeps pushing policies to private carriers at higher premiums.
- The industry's 2027 ask is defensive: keep tort reform, keep Citizens' eligibility tightening, and secure reinsurance support from the state. The gainers are carriers and reinsurers; the losers are policyholders forced off Citizens into pricier takeout offers.

- No source retrieved on AFP Action's statement.

#### Key question 6: Airbnb's Florida agenda

##### Takeaway
Airbnb's long running Florida ask is statewide preemption of local short term rental regulation. The Senate vehicle most recently discussed in detail is SB 280; the search surfaced explainers on it and a CBS Miami piece on a vacation rental proposal moving in the Senate, but I could not establish the 2026 bill status.

##### Cited Findings
- SB 280 is the vacation rental bill explainers describe as the statewide framework for hosts, [BnbCalc on SB 280](https://www.bnbcalc.com/blog/housing-market/government-policy/florida-short-term-rentals-an-explanation-of-the-florida-senate-bill-280)
- A vacation rental proposal was "on the move in Senate", [CBS Miami](https://www.cbsnews.com/miami/news/vacation-rental-proposal-on-the-move-in-senate)
- 2026 host guides summarize the current state rules, [Avantio](https://www.avantio.com/blog/florida-vacation-rental-laws/); [Awning](https://awning.com/post/florida-short-term-rental-laws)

##### Inferences
- My recollection, flagged as unverified here, is that DeSantis vetoed SB 280 in June 2024. If so, Airbnb's $1 million (round one) is a bet that a Donalds governor would sign a preemption bill DeSantis would not. The committee routing is through Senate Regulated Industries and House Commerce; both are controlled by leadership aligned with Donalds via Perez.

##### Gaps
- Could not confirm the 2024 veto, the 2025 or 2026 refiled bill numbers, their sponsors, or the committee votes.

#### Key question 7: Daniel Perez and legislative leadership

##### Takeaway
House Speaker Daniel Perez led 63 House members in endorsing Donalds and his political committees gave Donalds' committee $1 million; Perez also endorsed Joe Gruters for CFO. Donalds' war chest exceeded $31 million by the time Florida Politics tallied it, with reporting on billionaire out of state cash.

##### Cited Findings
- Perez led 63 House members endorsing Donalds, [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/760712-daniel-perez-leads-63-house-members-in-endorsing-byron-donalds-for-governor/)
- Perez's political committees gave $1 million to Donalds' committee, [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/754397-daniel-perez-political-committees-give-1m-boost-to-byron-donalds-campaign-for-governor/); [Miami Times](https://www.miamitimesonline.com/news/florida/daniel-perez-political-committees-give-1m-boost-to-byron-donalds-campaign-for-governor/article_8eab53e4-a074-412b-80fa-c2d4cc22cd7d.html)
- Donalds' war chest grew to more than $31 million, [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/758955-byron-donalds-war-chest-grows-to-more-than-31m/)
- Donalds' campaign was "flooded with billionaire cash from outside the state", [Bradenton Times](https://thebradentontimes.com/stories/untitled,204127)
- Perez endorsed Joe Gruters for CFO, [Yahoo / Herald-Tribune](https://www.yahoo.com/news/florida-house-speaker-daniel-perez-192212640.html)
- Perez raised nearly $1.4 million for an unopposed HD 116 reelection as redistricting neared, [Florida Politics](https://floridapolitics.com/archives/463381-daniel-perez-nears-1-4m-raised-for-unopposed-hd-116-reelection-as-redistricting-nears/)
- The House's December 2025 property tax cut plan is the Perez House's signature item, [Axios Tampa Bay, Dec 9, 2025](https://www.axios.com/local/tampa-bay/2025/12/09/how-the-florida-house-wants-to-cut-property-taxes)

##### Inferences
- Perez's House authored the property tax package that became Amendment 3, feuded with DeSantis through 2025, and is backing Donalds as the governor who will sign the House agenda (property tax, mid decade redistricting, and the Live Local and growth bills) that DeSantis vetoed or slow walked. The $1 million is leadership buying a cooperative governor, and Donalds' 63 House endorsements mean a 2027 session where the House and governor are aligned against the Senate.
